\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Interface class for \textquotesingle{}dcmsr\textquotesingle{} (DICOM Structured Reporting Documents). This class supports reading, writing, creation, printing and rendering of DICOM Structured Reporting (SR) documents. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} list of supported SOP classes is available in file "{}dsrtypes.\+h"{}. Also see \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a6545d88cf751a0b97c56c509dc644b85}{DSRTypes::\+\+E\+\_\+\+Document\+Type}.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_aa11d4f2c8419c769db5525560b411961} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+change\+Document\+Type (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_d_s_r_types_a6545d88cf751a0b97c56c509dc644b85}{E\+\_\+\+Document\+Type}}}]{document\+Type}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

change the type of the current document. Please note that only the type of the SR document is changed (if possible) but no new SOP instance is created. If needed, this has to be done with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a26f731b26c1b62d57f41ded386813edf}{create\+New\+SOPInstance()} in addition to this method or by calling \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_accd1c87b2236af57019750d492234001}{create\+New\+Document()} instead of this method. Before changing the document type, it is checked whether the currently stored document tree complies with the relationship content constraints of the new SR IOD. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em document\+Type} & new type of the SR document (see \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a6545d88cf751a0b97c56c509dc644b85}{DSRTypes::\+\+E\+\_\+\+Document\+Type}) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

check the given dataset before reading. This methods checks whether the dataset contains at least the DICOM attributes SOP class UID and modality. Any incorrectness regarding these two attributes is reported to the logger. Currently unsupported SOP classes are also reported as an error. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dataset} & DICOM dataset to be checked \\
\hline
{\em document\+Type} & SR document type retrieved from the SOP class UID \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{completeDocument()}{completeDocument()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [1/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_af04a81cd4a7bf131b792f83fa04a444b} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+complete\+Document (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

complete the current document. Sets the completion flag to COMPLETE if not already done (fails otherwise). This flag describes the estimated degree of completeness of an SR Document (see DICOM standard for details). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} completion flag description is set to an empty string (i.\+e. absent in DICOM dataset). \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a7f7ab2c73759ad6cd52e87add5645955}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!completeDocument@{completeDocument}}
\index{completeDocument@{completeDocument}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{completeDocument()}{completeDocument()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [2/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a7f7ab2c73759ad6cd52e87add5645955} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+complete\+Document (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{description}{, }\item[{const OFBool}]{check}{ = {\ttfamily OFTrue}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

complete the current document and set a completion description. Sets the completion flag to COMPLETE if not already done (fails otherwise). This flag describes the estimated degree of completeness of an SR Document (see DICOM standard for details). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} completion flag description can be modified independently from the flag by means of the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_abece6fde450ec7a1bab7383b2b7a30e5}{set\+Completion\+Flag\+Description()} -\/ see above. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em description} & explanation of the value set for completion flag. (optional, see previous method, VR=LO) \\
\hline
{\em check} & check \textquotesingle{}description\textquotesingle{} for conformance with VR and VM if enabled \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

create a new series. After generating a new series instance UID the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a26f731b26c1b62d57f41ded386813edf}{create\+New\+SOPInstance()} is called, i.\+e. also a new SOP instance UID is generated. This is a requirement of the DICOM standard. All other series-\/related attributes are cleared. \Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a283a3fa84572dc98418f27e108485c0c}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!createNewSeriesInStudy@{createNewSeriesInStudy}}

create a new series within a given study. After generating a new series instance UID within the given study the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a26f731b26c1b62d57f41ded386813edf}{create\+New\+SOPInstance()} is called, i.\+e. also a SOP instance UID is generated. This is a requirement of the DICOM standard. NB:\+ There is no mechanism that makes sure that the study-\/related attributes are consistent for all series of a study. This either has to be done manually or \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_acb772b634833230d355e7dda3b52ae46}{read\+Study\+Data()} should be used in combination with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a3a1d9e6b04e7ed035fd1582dc2719ea0}{create\+New\+Series()}. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em study\+UID} & study instance UID to be set (should be a valid UID) \\
\hline
{\em check} & check \textquotesingle{}study\+UID\textquotesingle{} for conformance with VR and VM if enabled \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a26f731b26c1b62d57f41ded386813edf} 
virtual void DSRDocument::\+create\+New\+SOPInstance (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

create a new SOP instance. Generate a new SOP instance UID, set the instance creation date/\+time and reset the finalized flag (OFFalse). This method is used internally for \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_accd1c87b2236af57019750d492234001}{create\+New\+Document()}, \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_afb9befcb88cf87d6325116508c7fa314}{create\+Revised\+Version()} and during object initialization. It could also be used explicitly from the calling application if a new UID should be created (this is the case if the study instance UID or series instance UID has changed as well as any other attribute within the SR Document General Module or SR Document Content Module, see DICOM standard for details). This method also updates other DICOM header attributes (by calling \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_abced8f7508302ab189237a83e79344f5}{update\+Attributes()}). However, instance-\/level attributes other than the SOP instance UID are usually not changed (if already set), e.\+g. the current instance number or content date/\+time. \Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a203fc10e7fd155f4da4eb7146668abbe}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!createNewStudy@{createNewStudy}}
\index{createNewStudy@{createNewStudy}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a203fc10e7fd155f4da4eb7146668abbe} 
virtual void DSRDocument::\+create\+New\+Study (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

create new study. After generating a new study instance UID the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a3a1d9e6b04e7ed035fd1582dc2719ea0}{create\+New\+Series()} is called, i.\+e. also a new series instance UID and SOP instance UID are generated. This is a requirement of the DICOM standard. All other study-\/related attributes are cleared (including the attributes from the Patient Study Module). \Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_afb9befcb88cf87d6325116508c7fa314}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!createRevisedVersion@{createRevisedVersion}}
\index{createRevisedVersion@{createRevisedVersion}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{createRevisedVersion()}{createRevisedVersion()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_afb9befcb88cf87d6325116508c7fa314} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+create\+Revised\+Version (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const OFBool}]{clear\+List}{ = {\ttfamily OFTrue}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

create a revised version of the current document. A revised version can only be created if the current document is already completed (see completion flag). If so, a reference to the current document is added to the predecessor documents sequence. If all revised versions of a SR document are stored (written to datasets/\+files) it is possible to trace back the full chain of previous versions. A new SOP instance is created and the content date/\+time are set automatically. Furthermore, the verifying observer and identical documents sequence are deleted, the verification flag is set to UNVERIFIED, the completion flag is set to PARTIAL (i.\+e. not complete), the completion flag description is deleted, all digital signatures contained in the document tree are deleted and the finalized flag is reset (OFFalse). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} preliminary flag is not modified by this method. Also the various lists of referenced instances remain unchanged, i.\+e. they have to be adapted manually if needed. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em clear\+List} & clear list of predecessor documents before adding the current document if OFTrue. Append current document to existing list otherwise. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a60c3880ffa4839074650c29ca9fe6696}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!finalizeDocument@{finalizeDocument}}
\index{finalizeDocument@{finalizeDocument}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{finalizeDocument()}{finalizeDocument()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a60c3880ffa4839074650c29ca9fe6696} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+finalize\+Document (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

finalize the current state of the document. A new document is originally not finalized but can be finalized using this method. This internal flag is e.\+g. used to indicate whether the entire document is digitally signed and, therefore, each newly added verifying observer would corrupt all previous signatures. NB:\+ A document needs to be completed first in order to be finalized. Some of the above document management functions do reset the flag (i.\+e. set the Finalized\+Flag to OFFalse), other methods (e.\+g. set\+XXX) do not change the flag though the state of the document is not finalized any more after they have been called. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module since there is no completion flag attribute in this module. Please note that this method has nothing to do with the preliminary flag. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

get list of referenced SOP instances (Current Requested Procedure Evidence). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} DICOM standard states:\+ "{}\+The intent of the Current Requested Procedure Evidence Sequence is to reference all evidence created in order to satisfy the current Requested Procedure(s) for this SR Document.  This shall include, but is not limited to, all current evidence referenced in the content tree."{} and "{}\+For a completed SR Document satisfying (i.\+e., being the final report for) the current Requested Procedure(s), this sequence shall list the full set of Composite SOP Instances created for the current Requested Procedure(s).  For other \+SOP Instances that include the SR Document General Module, this sequence shall contain at minimum the set of Composite SOP Instances from the current Requested Procedure(s) that are referenced in the content tree."{} and "{}\+In the context of the Key Object Selection, the current evidence is considered to be only the set of instances referenced within the Key \+Object Selection."{} \begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list object 
\end{DoxyReturn}

get list of identical documents. Please note that currently the user is responsible for filling and modifying the content of this list. However, the list is automatically cleared when a new instance is created (incl. a revised version of the current document). Possibly, there will be a create\+Duplicate() method or the like in the future which creates an identical copy of the current document in a new study/\+series. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} DICOM standard states:\+ "{}\+If identical copies of an SR Document are to be included in multiple Studies then the entire SR Document shall be duplicated with appropriate changes for inclusion into the different Studies (i.\+e. Study Instance UID, Series Instance UID, SOP \+Instance UID, Identical Documents Sequence etc.).  The Identical Documents Sequence Attribute in each SOP Instance shall contain references to all other duplicate SOP Instances."{} \begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list object 
\end{DoxyReturn}

get list of referenced SOP instances (Pertinent Other Evidence). \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} DICOM standard states:\+ "{}\+The Pertinent Other Evidence Sequence attribute is used to reference all other evidence considered pertinent for this SR Document that is not listed in the Current Requested Procedure Evidence Sequence.  This requires that the same SOP \+Instance shall not be referenced in both of these Sequences."{} \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list object 
\end{DoxyReturn}
\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a7a09de950cb8c1ba517f59581e84e3ba}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!getPredecessorDocuments@{getPredecessorDocuments}}

virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_d_s_r_s_o_p_instance_reference_list}{DSRSOPInstance\+Reference\+List}} \& DSRDocument::\+get\+Predecessor\+Documents (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

get list of predecessor documents. A document can have more than one (direct) predecessor document. This is e.\+g. the case when two or more documents have been merged to create it. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} corresponding method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_afb9befcb88cf87d6325116508c7fa314}{create\+Revised\+Version()} automatically adds a reference to the current document. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} DICOM standard states:\+ "{}\+[\+The Predecessor Documents Sequence]\+ Shall refer to SR SOP \+Instances (e.\+g. prior or provisional reports) whose content has been wholly or partially included in this document with or without modification."{} and "{}\+[...]\+ the use of the \+Predecessor Document Sequence allows tracing back to the input SR Document, which in this case is the previous version."{} \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list object 
\end{DoxyReturn}

virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_d_s_r_referenced_instance_list}{DSRReferenced\+Instance\+List}} \& DSRDocument::\+get\+Referenced\+Instances (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

get list of referenced SOP instances significantly related to the current SOP instance. However, the same instances shall not be referenced in the Current Requested Procedure Evidence Sequence, Pertinent Other Evidence Sequence, Predecessor Documents Sequence or Identical Documents Sequence. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} DICOM standard states:\+ "{}\+Such referenced Instances may include equivalent documents or renderings of this document. \+[...]\+ Required if the identity of a CDA Document equivalent to the current SOP Instance is known at the time of creation of this SOP instance. May be present otherwise."{} \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} Purpose of Reference Code should be taken from Defined Context Group 7006 (SR Document Purposes of Reference). Note:\+ An equivalent rendering of the document might be provided as an "{}\+Encapsulated PDF"{} DICOM object. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to list object 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a7d2d42c00b752795ef6b73514f2aa6e6}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!getVerifyingObserver@{getVerifyingObserver}}
\index{getVerifyingObserver@{getVerifyingObserver}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{getVerifyingObserver()}{getVerifyingObserver()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [1/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a7d2d42c00b752795ef6b73514f2aa6e6} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+get\+Verifying\+Observer (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const size\+\_\+t}]{idx}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{date\+Time}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{observer\+Name}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_d_s_r_coded_entry_value}{DSRCoded\+Entry\+Value}} \&}]{observer\+Code}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{organization}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

get information about a verifying observer. All reference variables are cleared before the information is retrieved, i.\+e. if an error occurs (return value != EC\+\_\+\+Normal) non-\/empty variables do contain valid (empty) data. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em idx} & index of the verifying observer to be retrieved (starting with 1). Use \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_ac88db33af78e5fb283ec1ae6fd7bcc48}{get\+Number\+Of\+Verifying\+Observers()} to get the maximum value. \\
\hline
{\em date\+Time} & reference to variable where the date and time when this document has been verified should be stored (required) \\
\hline
{\em observer\+Name} & reference to variable where the name of the person who has verified this document should be stored (required) \\
\hline
{\em observer\+Code} & reference to variable where the observer code should be stored. code identifying the verifying observer (optional, see previous method) \\
\hline
{\em organization} & reference to variable where the name of the organization to which the observer belongs should be stored (required) \\
\hline

get information about a verifying observer. All reference variables are cleared before the information is retrieved, i.\+e. if an error occurs (return value != EC\+\_\+\+Normal) non-\/empty variables do contain valid (empty) data. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em idx} & index of the verifying observer to be retrieved (starting with 1). Use \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_ac88db33af78e5fb283ec1ae6fd7bcc48}{get\+Number\+Of\+Verifying\+Observers()} to get the maximum value. \\
\hline
{\em date\+Time} & reference to variable where the date and time when this document has been verified should be stored (required) \\
\hline
{\em observer\+Name} & reference to variable where the name of the person who has verified this document should be stored (required) \\
\hline
{\em organization} & reference to variable where the name of the organization to which the observer belongs should be stored (required) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

check whether the document is finalized. A new document is originally not finalized but can be finalized using the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a60c3880ffa4839074650c29ca9fe6696}{finalize\+Document()}. This flag is e.\+g. used to indicate whether the entire document is digitally signed and, therefore, each newly added verifying observer would corrupt all previous signatures. \begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
OFTrue if finalized, OFFalse otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_af1aa84ec902ef171bd9226090b554440}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!read@{read}}
\index{read@{read}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{read()}{read()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_af1aa84ec902ef171bd9226090b554440} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+read (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_dcm_item}{Dcm\+Item}} \&}]{dataset}{, }\item[{const size\+\_\+t}]{flags}{ = {\ttfamily 0}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

read SR document from DICOM dataset. Please note that the current document is also deleted if the reading process fails. If logging is enabled, the reason for any error might be obtained from the log output. Also warning and debug messages are reported if the respective logger is enabled. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dataset} & reference to DICOM dataset from which the document should be read \\
\hline
{\em flags} & optional flag used to customize the reading process (see DSRTypes::\+\+RF\+\_\+xxx). E.\+g. \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a83753b88213566d65d311bfe987e1eef}{DSRTypes::\+\+RF\+\_\+read\+Digital\+Signatures} indicates whether to read the digital signatures from the dataset or not. If set, the MACParameters\+Sequence and the Digital\+Signatures\+Sequence are read for the general document header (equivalent to top-\/level content item) and each content item of the document tree. If not removed manually (with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_tree_aaa618c12a1d54ef262ede02883d0ba0e}{DSRDocument\+Tree::\+remove\+Signatures()}), the signatures are written back to the dataset when the \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a5fcdf74a96e5b75d8180bf22a8512136}{write()} method is called. Please note that the two signature sequences for any other sequence (e.\+g. Verifying\+Observer or Predecessor\+Documents) are never read. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

virtual void DSRDocument::\+remove\+Verification (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}{}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

remove verification information. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} list of verifying observers is cleared, the verification flag is set to UNVERIFIED and the finalized flag is reset (set to OFFalse). Normally, there should be no need to call this method. On the other hand, it is useful to guarantee a consistent state when processing documents which have not been created with this module/\+toolkit. \Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a67ef0d81f5e0f00884511f3967567e20}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!renderHTML@{renderHTML}}

render current SR document in HTML/\+\+XHTML format. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} output format is identical to that of the dsr2html command line tool. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em stream} & output stream to which the HTML/\+\+XHTML document is written \\
\hline
{\em flags} & optional flag used to customize the output (see DSRTypes::\+\+HF\+\_\+xxx) \\
\hline
{\em style\+Sheet} & optional filename/\+\+URL of a Cascading Style Sheet (CSS) \\

set specific character set. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} internal enumerated value is set accordingly. Please note that this method does not return an error if the given \textquotesingle{}value\textquotesingle{} is not defined by the DICOM standard or not supported by this class, e.\+g. when no mapping to the character set names needed for HTML/\+\+XHTML or XML output is defined. If needed, check the return value of the method \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a2a623ac55949b5e841c14638094c310c}{get\+Specific\+Character\+Set\+Type()} for \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a22d6fc0b263ca97faa2eb9052fce8054a0ef9cd0a3e505f8d1b4a95970ac599c3}{DSRTypes::\+\+CS\+\_\+invalid} or \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a22d6fc0b263ca97faa2eb9052fce8054a63e7c00f267c4e6baf6cf37663c6dbab}{DSRTypes::\+\+CS\+\_\+unknown} after calling this method. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em value} & value to be set (single or multiple values) or "{}"{} for no value \\
\hline
{\em check} & check \textquotesingle{}value\textquotesingle{} for conformance with VR (CS) and VM (1-\/n) if enabled \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

set document tree. Replace the currently stored document tree with the given one. Please note that the given \textquotesingle{}tree\textquotesingle{} is checked before setting it, i.\+e. only a valid document tree is accepted. However, a new SOP instance is never created. If needed, this has to be done with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a26f731b26c1b62d57f41ded386813edf}{create\+New\+SOPInstance()} in addition to or with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_accd1c87b2236af57019750d492234001}{create\+New\+Document()} before this method is called. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em tree} & document tree to be set (content will be copied) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

set document tree from root template. Replace the currently stored document tree with the one from the given root template. By default, this method expands the tree, i.\+e. instances of \doxylink{class_d_s_r_included_template_tree_node}{DSRIncluded\+Template\+Tree\+Node} that were added to the tree with \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_sub_tree_a3dff3e2695cf802e0e166774b1702041}{DSRDocument\+Sub\+Tree::\+include\+Template()} are replaced by their content, i.\+e. by the internally managed subtree. Please note that the additional comments on the above \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a4b427e873ab519bdf6d8a3b8c27d09db}{set\+Tree()} method also apply. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em root\+Template} & template specifying the document tree to be set (content will be copied). This parameter cannot be "{}const"{} because of \doxylink{class_d_s_r_root_template_a6dbb8b5111961ed266369b664cff1150}{DSRRoot\+Template::\+get\+Tree()}, which is called internally and which modifies the tree. \\
\hline
{\em expand\+Tree} & optional flag that allows for disabling the expanding of the document tree before setting it. Please note that various output methods like \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a5fcdf74a96e5b75d8180bf22a8512136}{write()} or \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_a67ef0d81f5e0f00884511f3967567e20}{render\+HTML()} do not yet work on such trees. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\Hypertarget{class_d_s_r_document_a717128b43055f03444fbc15f86e24d90}\index{DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}!verifyDocument@{verifyDocument}}
\index{verifyDocument@{verifyDocument}!DSRDocument@{DSRDocument}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{verifyDocument()}{verifyDocument()}\hspace{0.1cm}{\footnotesize\ttfamily [1/2]}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a717128b43055f03444fbc15f86e24d90} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+verify\+Document (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{observer\+Name}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_d_s_r_coded_entry_value}{DSRCoded\+Entry\+Value}} \&}]{observer\+Code}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{organization}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{date\+Time}{ = {\ttfamily "{}"{}}, }\item[{const OFBool}]{check}{ = {\ttfamily OFTrue}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

verify the current document by a specific observer. A document can be verified more than once. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} observer information is added to a sequence stored in the dataset. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} verification flag is automatically set to VERIFIED (if not already done) and the finalized flag is reset (set to OFFalse). Please note that only completed documents (see completion flag) can be verified and that a new SOP instance UID has to be generated (manually) according to the DICOM standard when creating a dataset/\+file from this document. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Not applicable to document types that use the Key Object Document Module. 
\end{DoxyNote}

\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em observer\+Name} & name of the person who has verified this document (required, VR=PN) \\
\hline
{\em observer\+Code} & code identifying the verifying observer (optional, see previous method) \\
\hline
{\em organization} & name of the organization to which the observer belongs (required, VR=LO) \\
\hline
{\em date\+Time} & verification date time (optional). If empty/\+absent the current date and time are used. \\
\hline
{\em check} & check \textquotesingle{}observer\+Name\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}observer\+Code\textquotesingle{}, \textquotesingle{}organization\textquotesingle{} and \textquotesingle{}date\+Time\textquotesingle{} for conformance with VR and VM if enabled \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

A document can be verified more than once. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} observer information is added to a sequence stored in the dataset. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} verification flag is automatically set to VERIFIED (if not already done) and the finalized flag is reset (set to OFFalse). Please note that only completed documents (see completion flag) can be verified and that a new SOP instance UID has to be generated (manually) according to the DICOM standard when creating a dataset/\+file from this document. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{write()}{write()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{class_d_s_r_document_a5fcdf74a96e5b75d8180bf22a8512136} 
virtual \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRDocument::\+write (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_dcm_item}{Dcm\+Item}} \&}]{dataset}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_dcm_stack}{Dcm\+Stack}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{marked\+Items}{ = {\ttfamily NULL}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [virtual]}}

write current SR document to DICOM dataset. Please note that the Content\+Template\+Sequence for the root content item is not written automatically for particular SOP Classes (e.\+g. Key Object Selection Document). Instead, the template identification has to be set manually for the root CONTAINER (see \doxylink{class_d_s_r_document_tree_node_a30d17a31806a60d6b72a0938ff8259cb}{DSRDocument\+Tree\+Node::\+set\+Template\+Identification()}). This is because the template constraints cannot be checked yet. If logging is enabled, warning and debug messages are reported to the log output. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dataset} & reference to DICOM dataset to which the current document should be written. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} \textquotesingle{}dataset\textquotesingle{} is not cleared before writing to it! \\
\hline
{\em marked\+Items} & optional stack where pointers to all \textquotesingle{}marked\textquotesingle{} content items (DICOM datasets/\+items) are added to during the write process. Can be used to digitally sign parts of the document tree. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}
